Land Raider and Spartan choices will become selectable when their vehicle entries are added.

 RULE hq-centurion-std | Standard Wargear | hidden=false | Power Armour, Bolt Pistol, Chainsword and Frag Grenades.
 RULE hq-centurion-la | Legiones Astartes | hidden=false | The model has the Legiones Astartes special rule, also has And They Shall Know No Fear as presented in ProHammer Classic, and is not Fearless unless another rule says otherwise.
 RULE hq-centurion-ic | Independent Character | hidden=false | Follows the Independent Character rules in ProHammer Classic.
 RULE hq-centurion-frag | Frag Grenades | hidden=false | Uses the normal ProHammer Frag Grenade and Assault Grenade rules. Against vehicles, Frag Grenades have Strength 4 + D6 Armour Penetration.
 RULE hq-centurion-consulrule | Legion Consuls | hidden=false | A Legion Centurion may be upgraded to no more than one Consul type. A Consul retains the Centurion profile, wargear, special rules and Armoury access unless stated otherwise. Wargear granted by a Consul is included in its upgrade cost and does not count toward the Armoury allowance. Consul restrictions override normal Centurion options.
 RULE hq-centurion-armoury-rule | Space Marine Armoury | hidden=false | The Centurion may select up to 100 points of weapons and wargear from the Space Marine Armoury. Armour, Mobility, Weapon Replacements and Additional Wargear all count toward the same 100-point limit. All normal Armoury restrictions apply. Consul-granted wargear is included in the Consul cost and does not count against this allowance.
